# Districts, Cities and Towns of India: RAG Directory

> Administrative vintage warning: district boundaries and names change. Verify against the National Portal and the relevant state government.
> Coverage warning: district lists are broad current-reference lists. The place field gives representative major cities/towns, not every village or settlement. For exhaustive 2011 town/village records, use the Census Location Code Directory linked in Sources.
